<h3 class="afd-title--custom-tag afd-title-big--bmargin-small afd-mobile-margin">
<a class="afd-title--black-link" href="/926952/it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu"><span itemprop="name"> “It Was Important for Both of us to Come Back Home”:  In Сonversation with Neri&amp;Hu</span>
</a></h3>
<meta content="2019-10-22" itemprop="datePublished"/>
<div class="date-publication afd-mobile-margin">
<time data-time-ago="2019-10-22T11:45:00+00:00" datetime="2019-10-22T11:45:00+00:00" lang="en" title="October 22">about 2 hours ago</time>
</div>
<div class="afd-post-content" itemprop="articleBody">
<figure aria-labelledby="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-5daed1d93312fd9f5d000039" class="featured-image js-image-size media-picture" data-height="580" data-width="578" id="newsroom-picture-att-id-5daed1d93312fd9f5d000039" style="width: 578px; height: 425.467px;">
<a class="js-image-size__link lazy-anchor" href="/926952/it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu" rel="attachment" style="width: 578px; height: 425.467px;">
<img alt=" “It Was Important for Both of us to Come Back Home”:  In Сonversation with Neri&amp;Hu, The Lantern. Image © Pedro Pegenaute" class="b-lazy b-loaded" data-nr-picture-id="5daed1d93312fd9f5d000039" itemprop="image" longdesc="https://www.archdaily.com/926952/it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu/5daed1d93312fd9f5d000039-it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu-photo" src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5dae/d1d9/3312/fd9f/5d00/0039/newsletter/Sulwhasoo_Flagship_Store_photographed_by_Pedro_Pegenaute_(4).jpg?1571738052"/>
</a>
<figcaption class="figcaption afd-desktop-e" id="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-5daed1d93312fd9f5d000039">
The Lantern. Image © Pedro Pegenaute
</figcaption>
<i class="figcaption-cc-icon afd-desktop-e"></i>
</figure>
<p></p><p class="p1">Lyndon Neri (b. 1965, Philippines) graduated from Harvard and Rossana Hu (b. 1968, Taiwan) from Princeton; they met while pursuing their undergraduate degrees at the University of California at Berkeley. Before establishing Neri&amp;Hu in 2004 in Shanghai, a prolific, multidisciplinary practice with over 100 international architects and designers, the husband-and-wife partners worked for a decade at Michael Graves &amp; Associates in Princeton. Among their most recognized works are The Waterhouse at South Bund in Shanghai, The Walled – Tsingpu Yangzhou Retreat, and Suzhou Chapel. Apart from running an architectural practice and design studio the partners lead a retail store Design Republic and serve as creative directors of a furniture brand Stellar Works. The following is an excerpt from our candid conversation at their Shanghai office.</p><p></p><p class="thumbs afd-desktop-e clearfix">
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="5daed1523312fd9f5d000036" href="/926952/it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu/5daed1523312fd9f5d000036-it-was-important-for-both-of-us-to-come-back-home-in-sonversation-with-neri-and-hu-photo" rel="attachment">

</a>

<h3 class="afd-title--custom-tag afd-title-big--bmargin-small afd-mobile-margin">

<p></p><p><em><a href="https://medium.com/sidewalk-talk/city-of-the-future-podcast-episode-7-stoa-retail-69885f4a410f?utm_medium=website&amp;utm_source=archdaily.com" target="_blank">City of the Future</a> is a bi-weekly podcast from <a href="/tag/sidewalk-labs">Sidewalk Labs</a> that explores ideas and innovations that will transform cities.</em></p><p></p><p></p><p>In the second episode from season 2, hosts Eric Jaffe and Vanessa Quirk discuss the future of retail with author Mark Pilkington, social entrepreneur Sarah Filley, <a href="/tag/sidewalk-labs">Sidewalk Labs</a>’ Director of Development Carrie Jackson, and others. </p><p></p>

<h3 class="afd-title--custom-tag afd-title-big--bmargin-small afd-mobile-margin">
<a class="afd-title--black-link" href="/926889/oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait"><span itemprop="name">OMA / Iyad Alsaka and Reinier De Graaf Uncover Conceptual Design for their First Project in Kuwait</span>
</a></h3>
<meta content="2019-10-22" itemprop="datePublished"/>
<div class="date-publication afd-mobile-margin">
<time data-time-ago="2019-10-22T09:30:00+00:00" datetime="2019-10-22T09:30:00+00:00" lang="en" title="October 22">about 4 hours ago</time>
</div>
<div class="afd-post-content" itemprop="articleBody">
<figure aria-labelledby="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-5dade1d33312fd2dbb000075" class="featured-image js-image-size media-picture" data-height="453" data-width="640" id="newsroom-picture-att-id-5dade1d33312fd2dbb000075" style="width: 640px; height: 300.113px;">
<a class="js-image-size__link lazy-anchor" href="/926889/oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait" rel="attachment" style="width: 640px; height: 300.113px;">
<img alt="OMA / Iyad Alsaka and Reinier De Graaf Uncover Conceptual Design for their First Project in Kuwait, Courtesy of OMA" class="b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="5dade1d33312fd2dbb000075" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5dad/e1d3/3312/fd2d/bb00/0075/newsletter/01_Wafra_Tower__Image_by_CGveron_(C)_OMA.jpg?1571676595" data-src-small="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5dad/e1d3/3312/fd2d/bb00/0075/medium_jpg/01_Wafra_Tower__Image_by_CGveron_(C)_OMA.jpg?1571676595" itemprop="image" longdesc="https://www.archdaily.com/926889/oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait/5dade1d33312fd2dbb000075-oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait-image" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAUEBAAAACwAAAAAAQABAAACAkQBADs="/>
</a>
<figcaption class="figcaption afd-desktop-e" id="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-5dade1d33312fd2dbb000075">
Courtesy of OMA
</figcaption>
<i class="figcaption-icon afd-desktop-e"></i>
</figure>
<p></p><p>Led by Iyad Alsaka, Reinier de Graaf, Jad Semaan, and Adrianne Fisher, <a href="/tag/oma">OMA</a>’s proposal was selected as the winning entry for a residential project on <a href="/tag/kuwait-city">Kuwait City</a>’s waterfront. In collaboration with local partner Kuwait-based consultant <a href="https://www.pace-me.com/?utm_medium=website&amp;utm_source=archdaily.com" target="_blank">Pace</a>, The Wafra tower will be OMA's first venture in the country upon its completion.</p><p></p><p class="thumbs afd-desktop-e clearfix">
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="5dade71e3312fd2dbb000076" href="/926889/oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait/5dade71e3312fd2dbb000076-oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait-image" rel="attachment">

</a>
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="5dadff3e3312fdd53c000395" href="/926889/oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait/5dadff3e3312fdd53c000395-oma-iyad-alsaka-and-reinier-de-graaf-uncover-conceptual-design-for-their-first-project-in-kuwait-image" rel="attachment">

<h3 class="afd-title--custom-tag afd-title-big--bmargin-small afd-mobile-margin">
<a class="afd-title--black-link" href="/926645/tab-2019-curator-yael-reisner-on-her-lifelong-fight-for-beauty"><span itemprop="name">TAB 2019: Curator Yael Reisner on her Lifelong Fight for Beauty</span>
</a></h3>
<meta content="2019-10-22" itemprop="datePublished"/>
<div class="date-publication afd-mobile-margin">
<time data-time-ago="2019-10-22T08:21:00+00:00" datetime="2019-10-22T08:21:00+00:00" lang="en" title="October 22">about 5 hours ago</time>
</div>
<div class="afd-post-content" itemprop="articleBody">
<div class="container-video">
<iframe allowfullscreen="" class="container-video__aspect-ratio" frameborder="0" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/6NUAd1xE8lU?theme=light&amp;showinfo=0&amp;color=white">
</iframe>
</div>
<p></p><p>Opened in the middle of September, <a href="/tag/tallinn-architecture-biennale">Tallinn Architecture Biennale</a> 2019 (TAB 2019) explores its theme "Beauty Matters" through all possible architectural means. Wood and textiles, 3D-printed structures and VR are only a few components of the main exhibition of the biennial, curated by Tel Aviv-born, London-based architect Yael Reisner.</p><p></p>
</div>

<h3 class="afd-title--custom-tag afd-title-big--bmargin-small afd-mobile-margin">
<a class="afd-title--black-link" href="/440979/happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook"><span itemprop="name">Spotlight: Peter Cook</span>
</a></h3>
<meta content="2019-10-22" itemprop="datePublished"/>
<div class="date-publication afd-mobile-margin">
<time data-time-ago="2019-10-22T07:30:00+00:00" datetime="2019-10-22T07:30:00+00:00" lang="en" title="October 22">about 6 hours ago</time>
</div>
<div class="afd-post-content" itemprop="articleBody">
<figure aria-labelledby="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-526482d1e8e44e88a00001ef" class="featured-image js-image-size media-picture" data-height="425" data-width="640" id="newsroom-picture-att-id-526482d1e8e44e88a00001ef" style="width: 640px; height: 281.563px;">
<a class="js-image-size__link lazy-anchor" href="/440979/happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook" rel="attachment" style="width: 640px; height: 281.563px;">
<img alt="Spotlight: Peter Cook, BIX Communicative Display Skin for the Kunsthaus Graz, 2003; View from Schlossberg. Image © Harry Schiffer / realities:united" class="b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="526482d1e8e44e88a00001ef" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5264/82d1/e8e4/4e88/a000/01ef/newsletter/BIX_Communicative_Display_Skin_for_the_Kunsthaus_Graz__2003__View_from_Schlossberg.jpg?1382318795" data-src-small="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5264/82d1/e8e4/4e88/a000/01ef/medium_jpg/BIX_Communicative_Display_Skin_for_the_Kunsthaus_Graz__2003__View_from_Schlossberg.jpg?1382318795" itemprop="image" longdesc="http://www.archdaily.com/89408/bix-light-and-media-facade-at-moma/" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAUEBAAAACwAAAAAAQABAAACAkQBADs="/>
</a>
<figcaption class="figcaption afd-desktop-e" id="figcaption_newsroom-picture-att-id-526482d1e8e44e88a00001ef">
BIX Communicative Display Skin for the Kunsthaus Graz, 2003; View from Schlossberg. Image © Harry Schiffer / realities:united
</figcaption>
<i class="figcaption-cc-icon afd-desktop-e"></i>
</figure>
<p></p><p>As one of the founding members of <a href="http://www.archdaily.com/tag/archigram/">Archigram</a>, the avant-garde neo-futurist architecture group of the 1960s, the British architect, professor, and writer Sir <a href="http://www.archdaily.com/office/peter-cook/" target="_blank">Peter Cook</a> (born 22 October 1936) has been a pivotal figure within the global architectural world for over half a century; one of his most significant works from his time with Archigram, <a href="http://www.archdaily.com/399329/ad-classics-the-plug-in-city-peter-cook-archigram/">The Plug-In City</a>, still invokes debates on technology and society, challenging standards of architectural discourse today.</p><p></p><p class="thumbs afd-desktop-e clearfix">
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="526481efe8e44ef4c2000205" href="http://www.archdaily.com/399329/ad-classics-the-plug-in-city-peter-cook-archigram/" rel="attachment">
<img alt="Plug-In City. Image © Peter Cook via the &lt;a href='http://archigram.westminster.ac.uk/'&gt;Archigram Archival Project&lt;/a&gt;" class="thumbs__img b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="526481efe8e44ef4c2000205" data-pin-nopin="true" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5264/81ef/e8e4/4ef4/c200/0205/thumb_jpg/51d71b74e8e44ed538000023_ad-classics-the-plug-in-city-peter-cook-archigram-_736_medium.jpg?1382318571" height="125" longdesc="http://www.archdaily.com/399329/ad-classics-the-plug-in-city-peter-cook-archigram/" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" title="Plug-In City. Image © Peter Cook via the &lt;a href='http://archigram.westminster.ac.uk/'&gt;Archigram Archival Project&lt;/a&gt;" width="125"/>
</a>
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="54477d54e58eceb5670000cc" href="http://www.archdaily.com/465030/abedian-school-of-architecture-crab-studio/" rel="attachment">
<img alt="Abedian School of Architecture / CRAB Studio. Image © Peter Bennetts" class="thumbs__img b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="54477d54e58eceb5670000cc" data-pin-nopin="true" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5447/7d54/e58e/ceb5/6700/00cc/thumb_jpg/52cdecd0e8e44e30c8000010_abedian-school-of-architecture-crab-studio_131008_bu_asa_1146.jpg?1413971274" height="125" longdesc="http://www.archdaily.com/465030/abedian-school-of-architecture-crab-studio/" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" title="Abedian School of Architecture / CRAB Studio. Image © Peter Bennetts" width="125"/>
</a>
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="54477d7ee58eceb5670000cd" href="http://www.archdaily.com/463741/departments-of-law-and-central-administration-crab-studio/" rel="attachment">
<img alt="Departments Of Law And Central Administration / CRAB Studio. Image © Ronald Kreimel" class="thumbs__img b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="54477d7ee58eceb5670000cd" data-pin-nopin="true" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5447/7d7e/e58e/ceb5/6700/00cd/thumb_jpg/52c9bd29e8e44eb4a300006e_departments-of-law-and-central-administration-crab-studio_crabstudio_128.jpg?1413971315" height="125" longdesc="http://www.archdaily.com/463741/departments-of-law-and-central-administration-crab-studio/" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" title="Departments Of Law And Central Administration / CRAB Studio. Image © Ronald Kreimel" width="125"/>
</a>
<a class="thumbs__link" data-nr-picture-id="5bcd9bf4f197cccd6300011f" href="/440979/happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook/5bcd9bf4f197cccd6300011f-happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook-image" rel="attachment">
<img alt="CLT Innovation Center for the Arts University Bournemouth / CRAB Studio. Image Courtesy of CRAB Studio" class="thumbs__img b-lazy" data-nr-picture-id="5bcd9bf4f197cccd6300011f" data-pin-nopin="true" data-src="https://images.adsttc.com/media/images/5bcd/9bf4/f197/cccd/6300/011f/thumb_jpg/Site_concept_diagram_CourtesyCrabStudio.jpg?1540201455" height="125" longdesc="https://www.archdaily.com/440979/happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook/5bcd9bf4f197cccd6300011f-happy-birthday-sir-peter-cook-image" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" title="CLT Innovation Center for the Arts University Bournemouth / CRAB Studio.
